- **compiler-frontend**: Use when building a lexer, Pratt or recursive-descent parser, AST, symbol table, type checker, or LLVM IR emitter for a language or DSL. Not for optimizing IR: use llvm-passes. ([OutlineDriven/odin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/OutlineDriven/odin-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/odin-native/skills/compiler-frontend) · ★38)
- **compiler-hardening**: Apply compiler hardening flags and sanitizers to C/C++ builds. Use when reviewing build configurations, CI pipelines, or Makefiles/CMakeLists for native code projects. ([RedHatProductSecurity/prodsec-skills](https://github.com/RedHatProductSecurity/prodsec-skills/tree/HEAD/module/skills/compiler-hardening) · ★52)
- **compiler-optimizations-deep**: Use when -O3 leaves a hot loop scalar, spills appear in assembly, or a PGO or BOLT deployment is planned or stalls. Not for machine lowering: use code-generation-and-backends. ([OutlineDriven/odin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/OutlineDriven/odin-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/odin-native/skills/compiler-optimizations-deep) · ★38)
- **compiler-toolchain**: Compiler toolchain development for custom processor ISAs — LLVM/GCC backend, assembler, linker scripts, runtime libraries, and regression validation. Use when building a compiler for a custom RISC-V extension, proprietary ISA, or any processor where no existing toolchain targets it correctly. ([hdl-tools/digital-chip-design-agents](https://github.com/hdl-tools/digital-chip-design-agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/compiler/skills/compiler-toolchain) · ★205)
- **compiling**: Compile and build the SkyWalking BanyanDB project. Use when the user asks to compile, build, or generate code for this project. ([apache/skywalking-banyandb](https://github.com/apache/skywalking-banyandb/tree/HEAD/skills/compiling) · ★352)
- **compiling-with-metal-shaderconverter**: ALWAYS use when compiling DXIL/HLSL to metallib with Metal shader converter (commonly referred to as MSC) via the metal-shaderconverter CLI or libmetalirconverter (IRCompiler, IRObject, IRShaderReflection) at runtime.
